follows me around, like I should have seen one more risk coming even though people keep telling me the controls were in place.\n- key: S076\n  name: Inability_Positive_Emotions\n  description: finish a difficult project milestone, and I know those things should feel good, but mostly I just feel flat and a little removed from it.\n- key: S078\n  name: Reckless_Self_Destructive_Behavior\n
